review_text: Found Steamers almost by accident and so glad we did. Tucked just behind the giant souvenir store on the main intersection in Gulf Shores, It was a Monday night and we sat on the patio (covered and screened) and were seated right away. Very comfortable and casual. The food was all very good. We had charcoal grilled oysters, which were excellent. Tried the Bushwhacker which was also excellent. The red snapper blackened was as good as any I’ve had and the corn, potatoes, and green beans were all very good. For the reviewers that downplayed the sides, the Butter Sauce on the table (and maybe a little salt, pepper, and hot sauce) made them all scrumptious. Service was great, and attentive. We plan to go back and bring our entire family.

id: 881bd2a1-b33d-41f0-8978-b4fc06d3233f
entity_slug: the-steamer-baked-oyster-bar
reviewer_name: Ashley Morikawa
rating: 4
body: Good place for large groups to sit inside or outside on the patio and have many options for seafood and also non-seafood options. We got a table pretty quickly for a group of 8. I really wanted baked oysters and they have an option to mix baked oyster flavors. I tried the cajun, mexican and parmesan. They were all tasty but the cajun with the sausage was my least favorite. I ordered a blackened scallops entree, but unfortunately, it was overcooked so I didn’t eat much of it. The rest of our group enjoyed their shrimp and steaks. Great sweet tea as well. And received very attentive service.

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 7c21f854-d408-4573-862c-6b78993a0f8f
entity_slug: the-steamer-baked-oyster-bar
reviewer_name: Mark Pillow
rating: 5
body: Found Steamers almost by accident and so glad we did. Tucked just behind the giant souvenir store on the main intersection in Gulf Shores, It was a Monday night and we sat on the patio (covered and screened) and were seated right away. Very comfortable and casual. The food was all very good. We had charcoal grilled oysters, which were excellent. Tried the Bushwhacker which was also excellent. The red snapper blackened was as good as any I’ve had and the corn, potatoes, and green beans were all very good. For the reviewers that downplayed the sides, the Butter Sauce on the table (and maybe a little salt, pepper, and hot sauce) made them all scrumptious. Service was great, and attentive. We plan to go back and bring our entire family.

approved: true
